Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the pde object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 342. The moduli space has dimension at most 168 with 1 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the pde space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 342 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 168. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the pde space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 555 and the convergence rate is O(753/n^3).
